 </head><body><main class="main-card"><div class="hero"><span class="hero-badge">Using points</span><h1>How to use Google Play Points and coupons</h1><p class="hero-meta">Updated 2026-07-25 ・ Play Points guide</p></div><article class="content"><div class="intro">Google Play Points can be exchanged for several kinds of rewards, but they do not all work the same way. A coupon discounts a qualifying purchase, Play credit becomes a separate Google Play balance, and an in-app item is delivered inside a specific app or game. Check the target, minimum purchase, expiration, and refund rule before you spend points.</div><nav class="intl-article-toc" aria-label="Table of contents"><h2>Table of contents</h2><ol><li><a href="#section-1">Start from the Use tab</a></li><li><a href="#section-2">Coupons discount only qualifying purchases</a></li><li><a href="#section-3">Play credit is not the same as a coupon</a></li><li><a href="#section-4">In-app items and partner rewards have separate rules</a></li><li><a href="#section-5">Compare usefulness, not only the displayed value</a></li></ol></nav><section class="section"><h2 id="section-1">Start from the Use tab</h2><p>Open Google Play, choose the Google account that owns the points, open Play Points, and select Use. The rewards shown there are the options currently available to that account and country.</p><p>Do not rely on an old screenshot or another region's reward list. Availability and required points can change.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-2">Coupons discount only qualifying purchases</h2><p>A coupon applies automatically when an in-app or in-game purchase meets its conditions. Check the eligible app, minimum purchase amount, discount amount, and expiration before exchanging points.</p><p>Google says coupons exchanged with points expire after one year. A saved coupon can be found in Notifications &amp; offers under Saved offers, although menu wording may vary by app version.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-3">Play credit is not the same as a coupon</h2><p>Google Play credit can be used for eligible Google Play purchases, while an app-specific coupon is limited to the named app or game and its conditions.</p><p>Credit received by exchanging points expires after one year. It is not cash and cannot be withdrawn to a bank or external wallet.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-4">In-app items and partner rewards have separate rules</h2><p>An exchanged in-app or in-game item must be found inside the relevant app. Partner vouchers, when available, are usually stored in the Perks tab and may have their own redemption deadline.</p><p>Google says points used for in-app items or donations generally cannot be refunded, so confirm the destination before completing the exchange.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-5">Compare usefulness, not only the displayed value</h2><p>The best reward is the one you were already going to use. A large-looking coupon can be poor value if it requires an extra purchase or expires before you need it.</p><p>Keep point-earning cost and redemption value separate. The calculator estimates how much spending may earn points; it does not decide which reward is worth using.</p></section><div class="cta-box"><h3>Check how much spending may earn the points you need</h3><p>Estimate the earning side first, then choose a reward only when you know where it will be used.</p><a class="cta-btn" href="/en/">Open the calculator</a></div><aside class="official-source-note"><h2>Official sources</h2><p>Google's official help explains the Use tab, coupons, Play credit, in-app items, partner rewards, expiration, and refund limitations.</p><p><a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/9079840?hl=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Use points in the Google Play Store</a></p><p><a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/9077247?hl=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Google Play Points troubleshooting</a></p></aside><section class="section"><h2>FAQ</h2><h3>Where is a coupon after I redeem it?</h3><p>Google says it is saved under Notifications &amp; offers in Saved offers. It should apply automatically to a qualifying in-app purchase.</p><h3>Do Play Points coupons expire?</h3><p>Yes. Google says coupons exchanged with points expire after one year.</p><h3>Can I undo an in-app item exchange?</h3><p>Google says points used for in-app or in-game items generally cannot be refunded.</p></section><section class="section related-links-section"><h2>Next step</h2><ul><li><a href="/en/articles/google-play-points-join-eligibility.html">Join requirements and missing Join button</a></li><li><a href="/en/articles/google-play-points-cash-conversion.html">Why Play credit is not cash</a></li><li><a href="/en/articles/google-play-points-expiration.html">Point and reward expiration</a></li><li><a href="/en/points-cost/">Points-to-cost calculator</a></li><li><a href="/author/katakata.html">Katakata</a></li></ul></section></article><aside class="author-box" aria-label="Article author"><p class="author-box-label">Article author</p><p class="author-box-name"><a href="/author/katakata.html" rel="author">Katakata</a> - Google Play Points Calculator</p><p>This guide separates official rules from practical planning. Confirm the current options and eligibility in the Google Play app before acting.</p></aside><footer class="article-footer"><p><a href="/en/articles/">Back to guides</a> | <a href="/en/">Calculator top</a></p>    <p class="site-footer-trademark">Google Play, the Google Play logo, and Android are trademarks of Google LLC. This website is an unofficial community calculator and guide, not affiliated with or endorsed by Google LLC or any game publishers mentioned.</p>

 </head><body><main class="main-card"><div class="hero"><span class="hero-badge">포인트 사용</span><h1>Google Play Points 사용법과 쿠폰 확인 방법</h1><p class="hero-meta">업데이트 2026-07-25 ・ Play Points 가이드</p></div><article class="content"><div class="intro">Google Play Points는 여러 리워드로 교환할 수 있지만 사용 방식은 서로 다릅니다. 쿠폰은 조건을 충족한 구매를 할인하고, Play 크레딧은 별도 Google Play 잔액이 되며, 인앱 아이템은 특정 앱이나 게임 안에서 받습니다. 포인트를 쓰기 전에 대상, 최소 결제액, 유효기간과 환불 조건을 확인하세요.</div><nav class="intl-article-toc" aria-label="목차"><h2>목차</h2><ol><li><a href="#section-1">먼저 사용 탭을 확인하세요</a></li><li><a href="#section-2">쿠폰은 조건을 충족한 구매만 할인합니다</a></li><li><a href="#section-3">Play 크레딧과 쿠폰은 다릅니다</a></li><li><a href="#section-4">인앱 아이템과 파트너 리워드는 규칙이 따로 있습니다</a></li><li><a href="#section-5">표시 금액보다 실제 사용 가능성을 비교하세요</a></li></ol></nav><section class="section"><h2 id="section-1">먼저 사용 탭을 확인하세요</h2><p>Google Play에서 포인트를 보유한 계정을 선택하고 Play Points의 사용 탭을 여세요. 현재 계정과 국가에서 실제로 이용할 수 있는 리워드가 표시됩니다.</p><p>예전 화면이나 다른 국가의 목록을 기준으로 교환 계획을 세우지 마세요. 제공 항목과 필요 포인트는 달라질 수 있습니다.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-2">쿠폰은 조건을 충족한 구매만 할인합니다</h2><p>쿠폰은 대상 인앱 또는 인게임 구매가 조건을 충족하면 자동 적용됩니다. 교환 전에 대상 앱, 최소 결제액, 할인액과 유효기간을 확인하세요.</p><p>Google은 포인트로 교환한 쿠폰이 1년 후 만료된다고 안내합니다. 저장한 쿠폰은 알림 및 혜택의 저장된 혜택에서 확인할 수 있으며 앱 버전에 따라 메뉴명이 다를 수 있습니다.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-3">Play 크레딧과 쿠폰은 다릅니다</h2><p>Play 크레딧은 대상 Google Play 구매에 사용할 수 있지만 앱 전용 쿠폰은 지정된 앱이나 게임과 조건에만 적용됩니다.</p><p>포인트로 교환한 Play 크레딧도 1년 후 만료됩니다. 현금이 아니므로 은행이나 외부 지갑으로 출금할 수 없습니다.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-4">인앱 아이템과 파트너 리워드는 규칙이 따로 있습니다</h2><p>교환한 인앱 또는 인게임 아이템은 해당 앱 안에서 확인해야 합니다. 제공되는 경우 파트너 쿠폰은 혜택 탭에 저장되며 별도 사용 기한이 있을 수 있습니다.</p><p>Google은 인앱 아이템이나 기부에 사용한 포인트는 일반적으로 환불되지 않는다고 안내하므로 교환 대상을 먼저 확인하세요.</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-5">표시 금액보다 실제 사용 가능성을 비교하세요</h2><p>가장 좋은 리워드는 원래 사용할 예정이었던 혜택입니다. 추가 결제를 요구하거나 사용 전에 만료되는 쿠폰은 큰 할인처럼 보여도 실용 가치가 낮을 수 있습니다.</p><p>포인트 적립에 필요한 비용과 교환 가치는 별개입니다. 계산기는 적립 예상만 돕고 어떤 리워드가 최선인지 결정하지 않습니다.</p></section><div class="cta-box"><h3>필요한 포인트를 모으는 데 필요한 금액 확인</h3><p>적립에 필요한 금액을 먼저 확인하고 실제로 사용할 리워드만 교환하세요.</p><a class="cta-btn" href="/ko/">계산기 열기</a></div><aside class="official-source-note"><h2>공식 출처</h2><p>Google 공식 도움말에서 사용 탭, 쿠폰, Play 크레딧, 인앱 아이템, 파트너 리워드와 유효기간·환불 제한을 확인할 수 있습니다.</p><p><a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/9079840?hl=ko" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Google Play 스토어에서 포인트 사용하기</a></p><p><a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/9077247?hl=ko" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Google Play Points 문제 해결</a></p></aside><section class="section"><h2>FAQ</h2><h3>교환한 쿠폰은 어디에서 확인하나요?</h3><p>Google 안내상 알림 및 혜택의 저장된 혜택에서 확인하며 조건을 충족한 인앱 구매에 자동 적용됩니다.</p><h3>Play Points 쿠폰에도 유효기간이 있나요?</h3><p>예. 포인트로 교환한 쿠폰은 1년 후 만료됩니다.</p><h3>인앱 아이템 교환을 취소할 수 있나요?</h3><p>Google 안내상 인앱 또는 인게임 아이템에 사용한 포인트는 일반적으로 환불되지 않습니다.</p></section><section class="section related-links-section"><h2>다음 단계</h2><ul><li><a href="/ko/articles/google-play-points-join-eligibility.html">가입 조건과 가입 버튼이 없을      </script>
 </head><body><main class="main-card"><div class="hero"><span class="hero-badge">使用點數</span><h1>Google Play Points 使用方式與優惠券確認</h1><p class="hero-meta">更新 2026-07-25 ・ Play Points 指南</p></div><article class="content"><div class="intro">Google Play Points 可以兌換多種獎勵，但使用方式並不相同。優惠券會折抵符合條件的購買，Play 抵用金會成為另一筆 Google Play 餘額，App 內項目則會出現在指定 App 或遊戲中。使用點數前，先確認適用對象、最低消費、期限與退款規則。</div><nav class="intl-article-toc" aria-label="目錄"><h2>目錄</h2><ol><li><a href="#section-1">先從「使用」頁籤開始</a></li><li><a href="#section-2">優惠券只折抵符合條件的購買</a></li><li><a href="#section-3">Play 抵用金和優惠券不是同一種獎勵</a></li><li><a href="#section-4">App 內項目與合作夥伴獎勵有各自規則</a></li><li><a href="#section-5">比較實際用途，不只看顯示價值</a></li></ol></nav><section class="section"><h2 id="section-1">先從「使用」頁籤開始</h2><p>在 Google Play 選擇持有點數的帳號，進入 Play Points 並開啟「使用」。畫面列出的內容才是目前帳號與地區可使用的獎勵。</p><p>不要依照舊截圖或其他地區的清單安排兌換。獎勵內容與所需點數可能改變。</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-2">優惠券只折抵符合條件的購買</h2><p>當 App 內或遊戲內購買符合條件時，優惠券會自動套用。兌換前請確認適用 App、最低消費、折扣金額與有效期限。</p><p>Google 說明指出，使用點數兌換的優惠券會在一年後到期。已儲存的優惠券可在「通知與優惠」的已儲存優惠中查看，選單名稱可能因 App 版本不同。</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-3">Play 抵用金和優惠券不是同一種獎勵</h2><p>Play 抵用金可用於符合資格的 Google Play 購買；App 專用優惠券則只適用於指定 App 或遊戲及其條件。</p><p>用點數兌換的 Play 抵用金也會在一年後到期。它不是現金，不能提領到銀行或外部電子錢包。</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-4">App 內項目與合作夥伴獎勵有各自規則</h2><p>兌換的 App 內或遊戲內項目要到對應 App 中查看。若帳號提供合作夥伴票券，通常會儲存在福利頁籤，並可能有另外的使用期限。</p><p>Google 表示，使用點數取得 App 內項目或進行捐款通常不能退款，因此完成前要確認兌換對象。</p></section><section class="section"><h2 id="section-5">比較實際用途，不只看顯示價值</h2><p>最好的獎勵是你原本就會使用的內容。需要額外消費或在使用前到期的優惠券，即使折扣看起來很大，也可能沒有實際價值。</p><p>累積點數的成本與兌換價值是兩件事。計算器只估算取得點數所需消費，不會替你決定哪個獎勵最划算。</p></section><div class="cta-box"><h3>確認取得所需點數要花多少</h3><p>先估算累積所需消費，再只兌換確定會使用的獎勵。</p><a class="cta-btn" href="/tw/">開啟計算機</a></div><aside class="official-source-note"><h2>官方來源</h2><p>Google 官方說明介紹「使用」頁籤、優惠券、Play 抵用金、App 內項目、合作夥伴獎勵，以及期限與退款限制。</p><p><a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/9079840?hl=zh-Hant" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">在 Google Play 商店使用點數</a></p><p><a href="https://support.google.com/googleplay/answer/9077247?hl=zh-Hant" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Google Play Points 問題排解</a></p></aside><section class="section"><h2>FAQ</h2><h3>兌換後的優惠券在哪裡？</h3><p>依 Google 說明，可在「通知與優惠」的已儲存優惠中查看，符合條件的 App 內購買會自動套用。</p><h3>Play Points 優惠券會到期嗎？</h3><p>會。使用點數兌換的優惠券會在一年後到期。</p><h3>可以取消 App 內項目的兌換嗎？</h3><p>依 Google 說明，使用點數取得 App 內或遊戲內項目通常不能退款。</p></section><section class="section related-links-section"><h2>下一步</h2><ul><li><a href="/tw/articles/google-play-points-join-eligibility.html">加入資格與沒有加入按鈕時</a></li><li><a href="/tw/articles/google-play-points-cash-conversion.html">為什麼 Play 抵用金不是現金</a></li><li><a href="/tw/articles/google-play-points-expiration.html">點數與獎勵有效期限</a></li><li><a href="/tw/points-cost/">點數需要多少消費計算器</a></li><li><a href="/author/katakata.html">Katakata</a></li></ul></section></article><aside class="author-box" aria-label="文章作者"><p class="author-box-label">文章作者</p><p class="author-box-name"><a href="/author/katakata.html" rel="author">Katakata</a> - Google Play Points 計算器</p><p>本文把官方規則與實際確認步驟分開整理。操作前請以 Google Play App 顯示的目前選項與資格為準。</p></aside><footer class="article-footer"><p><a href="/tw/articles/">返回指南</a> | <a href="/tw/">計算機首頁</a></p>    <p class="site-footer-trademark">Google Play、Google Play 標誌及 Android 均為     'use strict';
    
    (() => {
        if (window.PlayPointConsent) return;
    
        const analyticsCallbacks = new Set();
        const adCallbacks = new Set();
        const EEA_UK_CH_REGIONS = [
            'AT', 'BE', 'BG', 'CH', 'CY', 'CZ', 'DE', 'DK', 'EE', 'ES', 'FI', 'FR',
            'GB', 'GR', 'HR', 'HU', 'IE', 'IS', 'IT', 'LI', 'LT', 'LU', 'LV', 'MT',
            'NL', 'NO', 'PL', 'PT', 'RO', 'SE', 'SI', 'SK'
        ];
        const PURPOSE_STATUS = Object.freeze({ UNKNOWN: 0, GRANTED: 1, DENIED: 2, NOT_APPLICABLE: 3, NOT_CONFIGURED: 4 });
        const DENIED_STATE = Object.freeze({
            analytics_storage: 'denied',
            ad_storage: 'denied',
            ad_user_data: 'denied',
            ad_personalization: 'denied'
        });
    
        let analyticsStatus = 'pending';
        let adStatus = 'pending';
        let source = 'pending';
        let consentState = DENIED_STATE;
        let tcfListenerAttached = false;
        let timeoutId = null;
    
        // Google tags should read the CMP's TC string directly. This must be set before gtag calls.
        window.gtag_enable_tcf_support = true;
        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
        window.gtag = window.gtag || function gtag() {
            window.dataLayer.push(arguments);
        };
    
        // Outside regulated regions, keep the existing granted default. In EEA/UK/CH, default to denied
        // until Google Privacy & Messaging / TCF provides the user's choice.
        window.gtag('consent', 'default', {
            analytics_storage: 'granted',
            ad_storage: 'granted',
            ad_user_data: 'granted',
            ad_personalization: 'granted'
        });
        window.gtag('consent', 'default', {
            analytics_storage: 'denied',
            ad_storage: 'denied',
            ad_user_data: 'denied',
            ad_personalization: 'denied',
            region: EEA_UK_CH_REGIONS,
            wait_for_update: 500
        });
    
        function flush(callbacks, allowed) {
            if (!allowed) return;
            for (const callback of callbacks) {
                try { callback(); } catch (error) { console.error('同意後の処理に失敗しました。', error); }
            }
            callbacks.clear();
        }
    
        function dispatchStatus() {
            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('playpoint:consent-updated', {
                detail: {
                    status: analyticsStatus,
                    adStatus,
                    source,
                    consent: { ...consentState }
                }
            }));
        }
    
        function normalizePurposeStatus(value) {
            if (value === PURPOSE_STATUS.GRANTED || value === PURPOSE_STATUS.NOT_APPLICABLE || value === PURPOSE_STATUS.NOT_CONFIGURED) {
                return 'granted';
            }
            if (value === PURPOSE_STATUS.DENIED) return 'denied';
            return 'pending';
        }
    
        function applyConsentState(nextState, nextSource) {
            consentState = Object.freeze({ ...DENIED_STATE, ...nextState });
            source = nextSource;
            analyticsStatus = consentState.analytics_storage === 'granted'
                ? 'granted'
                : (consentState.analytics_storage === 'pending' ? 'pending' : 'denied');
            adStatus = consentState.ad_storage === 'granted'
                ? 'granted'
                : (consentState.ad_storage === 'pending' ? 'pending' : 'denied');
            if (analyticsStatus !== 'pending' || adStatus !== 'pending') {
                if (timeoutId !== null) window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                timeoutId = null;
            }
            flush(analyticsCallbacks, analyticsStatus === 'granted');
            flush(adCallbacks, adStatus === 'granted');
            dispatchStatus();
        }
    
        function readGoogleFcConsentMode() {
            if (!window.googlefc || typeof window.googlefc.getGoogleConsentModeValues !== 'function') return false;
            let values;
            try {
                values = window.googlefc.getGoogleConsentModeValues();
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('Google consent mode values could not be read.', error);
                return false;
            }
            if (!values) return false;
            applyConsentState({
                analytics_storage: normalizePurposeStatus(values.analyticsStoragePurposeConsentStatus),
                ad_storage: normalizePurposeStatus(values.adStoragePurposeConsentStatus),
                ad_user_data: normalizePurposeStatus(values.adUserDataPurposeConsentStatus),
                ad_personalization: normalizePurposeStatus(values.adPersonalizationPurposeConsentStatus)
            }, 'googlefc');
            return true;
        }
    
        function buildTcfFallbackState(tcData) {
            if (tcData.gdprApplies === false) {
                return {
                    analytics_storage: 'granted',
                    ad_storage: 'granted',
                    ad_user_data: 'granted',
                    ad_personalization: 'granted'
                };
            }
            const consents = tcData.purpose?.consents || {};
            const purpose1 = consents[1] === true;
            const purpose3 = consents[3] === true;
            const purpose4 = consents[4] === true;
            const purpose7 = consents[7] === true;
            return {
                analytics_storage: purpose1 ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                ad_storage: purpose1 ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                ad_user_data: purpose1 && purpose7 ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                ad_personalization: purpose1 && purpose3 && purpose4 ? 'granted' : 'denied'
            };
        }
    
        function handleTcfData(tcData, success) {
            if (!success || !tcData) return;
            if (!['tcloaded', 'useractioncomplete'].includes(tcData.eventStatus)) return;
            // Google Privacy & Messaging values are the preferred source because they already map
            // the CMP decision to Consent Mode purposes. TCF is a conservative fallback only.
            if (readGoogleFcConsentMode()) return;
            applyConsentState(buildTcfFallbackState(tcData), 'tcf-fallback');
        }
    
        function attachTcfListener() {
            if (tcfListenerAttached || typeof window.__tcfapi !== 'function') return false;
            tcfListenerAttached = true;
            window.__tcfapi('addEventListener', 2, handleTcfData);
            return true;
        }
    
        function registerGoogleFcListener() {
            window.googlefc = window.googlefc || {};
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue = window.googlefc.callbackQueue || [];
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue.push({
                CONSENT_MODE_DATA_READY() {
                    readGoogleFcConsentMode();
                }
            });
        }
    
        function waitForTcfApi() {
            if (attachTcfListener()) return;
            let attempts = 0;
            const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
                attempts += 1;
                if (attachTcfListener() || attempts >= 20) window.clearInterval(timer);
            }, 250);
        }
    
        function showSettings() {
            window.googlefc = window.googlefc || {};
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue = window.googlefc.callbackQueue || [];
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue.push({
                CONSENT_API_READY() {
                    if (typeof window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage === 'function') window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage();
                }
            });
            if (typeof window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage === 'function') window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage();
        }
    
        window.PlayPointConsent = Object.freeze({
            whenAnalyticsGranted(callback) {
                if (typeof callback !== 'function') return;
                if (analyticsStatus === 'granted') callback();
                else analyticsCallbacks.add(callback);
            },
            whenAdsAllowed(callback) {
                if (typeof callback !== 'function') return;
                if (adStatus === 'granted') callback();
                else adCallbacks.add(callback);
            },
            // Backward-compatible alias for analytics callers.
            whenGranted(callback) { this.whenAnalyticsGranted(callback); },
            getStatus() { return analyticsStatus; },
            getAdStatus() { return adStatus; },
            getConsentState() { return { ...consentState }; },
            getSource() { return source; },
            showSettings
        });
    
        registerGoogleFcListener();
        waitForTcfApi();
        // If both the GoogleFC and TCF APIs fail to become available, do not silently grant consent.
        // A late CMP callback can still recover from this denied state.
        timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
            if (analyticsStatus === 'pending' && adStatus === 'pending') applyConsentState(DENIED_STATE, 'timeout');
        }, 5000);
    
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('playpoint:consent-ready', {
            detail: { status: analyticsStatus, adStatus }
        }));
    })();

    (function () {
        'use strict';
    
        const GA_MEASUREMENT_ID = 'G-HED6D0FR4L';
        const ADSENSE_CLIENT = 'ca-pub-3845885843809455';
        let blogAdsenseLoaded = false;
        let consentManagerPromise = null;
        let analyticsCorePromise = null;
    
        const isArticlePageTop = window.location.pathname.includes('/articles/');
        const isBlogPage = window.location.pathname.includes('/blog');
        const isLatestPage = window.location.pathname.includes('/latest/');
        const rootPath = (isArticlePageTop || isBlogPage || isLatestPage) ? '../' : './';
    
        function ensureConsentManager() {
            if (window.PlayPointConsent) return Promise.resolve(window.PlayPointConsent);
            if (consentManagerPromise) return consentManagerPromise;
    
            consentManagerPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="/js/consent.js?v=fa7ca65a92"]');
                if (existing) {
                    existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointConsent), { once: true });
                    existing.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                    return;
                }
                const script = document.createElement('script');
                script.src = rootPath + 'js/consent.js?v=fa7ca65a92';
                script.async = true;
                script.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointConsent), { once: true });
                script.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                document.head.appendChild(script);
            });
            return consentManagerPromise;
        }
    
        function ensureAnalyticsCore() {
            if (window.PlayPointAnalytics) return Promise.resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics);
            if (analyticsCorePromise) return analyticsCorePromise;
    
            analyticsCorePromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="/js/analytics-core.js?v=a7babf5f72"]');
                if (existing) {
                    existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics), { once: true });
                    existing.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                    return;
                }
                const script = document.createElement('script');
                script.src = rootPath + 'js/analytics-core.js?v=a7babf5f72';
                script.async = true;
                script.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics), { once: true });
                script.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                document.head.appendChild(script);
            });
            return analyticsCorePromise;
        }
    
        function runAfterConsent(callback, purpose = 'analytics') {
            return Promise.all([ensureAnalyticsCore(), ensureConsentManager()])
                .then(() => {
                    const consent = window.PlayPointConsent;
                    if (purpose === 'ads' && typeof consent.whenAdsAllowed === 'function') {
                        consent.whenAdsAllowed(callback);
                        return;
                    }
                    if (typeof consent.whenAnalyticsGranted === 'function') {
                        consent.whenAnalyticsGranted(callback);
                        return;
                    }
                    consent.whenGranted(callback);
                })
                .catch((error) => console.error('Consent manager load failed:', error));
        }
    
        function getDisplayMode() {
            if (typeof window.matchMedia === 'function' && window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches) {
                return 'standalone';
            }
            if (window.navigator && window.navigator.standalone === true) {
                return 'standalone';
            }
            return 'browser';
        }
    
        // 記事ページにもブログで選んだテーマとカテゴリー配色を引き継ぐ
        function applyArticlePresentationSettings() {
            if (!window.location.pathname.includes('/articles/')) return;
    
            let theme = 'dark';
            try {
                const savedSettings = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('katakata_blog_settings') || '{}');
                if (savedSettings.theme === 'light' || savedSettings.theme === 'dark') {
                    theme = savedSettings.theme;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('ブログ設定を読み込めませんでした。既定テーマを使用します。', error);
            }
    
            const allowedCategories = ['ランク', 'トラブル', '使い方', 'キャンペーン'];
            const category = document.querySelector('meta[name="article:category"]')?.content;
            document.body.dataset.blogTheme = theme;
            document.body.dataset.articleCategory = allowedCategories.includes(category) ? category : '使い方';
        }
    
        // ブログと記事のイベントキューをGA4へ送れるよう、初期描画後に一度だけ読み込む
        function loadCommonAnalytics() {
            if (window.__playpointGaConfigured) return;
            window.__playpointGaConfigured = true;
            window.PlayPointAnalytics.installGtagBridge();
            window.gtag('js', new Date());
            window.gtag('set', { app_display_mode: getDisplayMode() });
            window.gtag('config', GA_MEASUREMENT_ID);
            window.PlayPointAnalytics.markAnalyticsReady();
    
            if (document.querySelector(`script[src*="googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_MEASUREMENT_ID}"]`)) return;
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.async = true;
            script.src = `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_MEASUREMENT_ID}`;
            script.onerror = () => console.error('Analytics load failed');
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        }
    
        function scheduleCommonAnalytics() {
            if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
                window.requestIdleCallback(() => void runAfterConsent(loadCommonAnalytics), { timeout: 2500 });
                return;
            }
            window.setTimeout(() => void runAfterConsent(loadCommonAnalytics), 1500);
        }
    
        function loadBlogAdsense() {
            if (blogAdsenseLoaded) return;
            blogAdsenseLoaded = true;
            if (document.querySelector('script[src*="pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js"]')) return;
    
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.async = true;
            script.src = `https://pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js?client=${ADSENSE_CLIENT}`;
            script.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
            script.onerror = () => {
                blogAdsenseLoaded = false;
                console.error('AdSense load failed');
            };
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        }
    
        function setupBlogAdsense() {
            if (!(isBlogPage || isArticlePageTop)) return;
            // Google Privacy & Messaging / TCFの初期化に必要な非同期ライブラリは早期取得する。
            // 手動広告枠のpushはarticle.js側でad_storage許可後にだけ実行する。
            loadBlogAdsense();
        }
    
        function ensureCommonStyles() {
            if (document.querySelector('link[data-common-components-style]')) return;
            const stylesheet = document.createElement('link');
            stylesheet.rel = 'stylesheet';
            stylesheet.href = rootPath + 'blog/common-components.css?v=bf40a1b867';
            stylesheet.dataset.commonComponentsStyle = 'true';
            document.head.appendChild(stylesheet);
        }
    
        // ===========================================
        // Common Header & Footer
        // ===========================================
        function renderCommonComponents() {
            // ページ固有のヘッダー・フッターがあれば、そのデザインを優先して重複生成しない
            const existingHeader = document.querySelector('header');
            const existingFooter = document.querySelector('footer');
    
            // Allow relative path adjustment if needed (default to absolute/root-relative for now based on file analysis)
            // Since files are in /articles/, links need to go up one level or use absolute URLs
            // The existing code used absolute URLs (https://playpoint-sim.com/), we will preserve that for consistency with current production
            // OR use relative paths if running locally. Let's stick to the existing absolute URLs as seen in the file,
            // BUT for local development, it might be better to handle relative paths.
            // Let's use relative paths for better local preview support, based on where the script is running.
    
            // --- Header Rendering ---
            if (!existingHeader) {
                const headerHTML = `
                <div class="header-inner">
                    <a class="logo" href="${rootPath}index.html">🎮 Playポイント計算機</a>
                    <nav class="nav">
                        <a href="${rootPath}blog/">📝 記事一覧</a>
                        <a href="https://katakatalab.com/">🧪 KatakataLab</a>
                    </nav>
                </div>
                `;
                const header = document.createElement('header');
                header.className = 'header';
                header.innerHTML = headerHTML;
                document.body.insertBefore(header, document.body.firstChild);
            }
    
            // --- Footer Rendering ---
            if (!existingFooter) {
                const footerHTML = `
                <p style="margin-bottom: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.8rem;">
                    <a href="${rootPath}privacy.html">プライバシーポリシー</a> ｜
                    <a href="${rootPath}terms.html">利用規約</a>
                </p>
                <p>© <span class="copyright-year">2024-${new Date().getFullYear()}</span> Playポイント計算機</p>
                `;
                const footer = document.createElement('footer');
                footer.className = 'site-footer';
                footer.innerHTML = footerHTML;
                document.body.appendChild(footer);
            }
        }
    
        // ===========================================
        // Auto Table of Contents (TOC)
        // ===========================================
        function generateTableOfContents() {
            const content = document.querySelector('.content, .main-content-column');
            if (!content || content.querySelector('.toc-box, .inpage-toc, .intl-article-toc, .toc-container, .article-toc, [aria-label*="目次"], [aria-label*="Table of contents"]')) return;
    
            // 主題となるセクションだけに絞り、CTAや補足見出しで目次を膨らませない
            const headings = Array.from(content.querySelectorAll('.section > h2, article > section > h2'))
                .filter(heading => !heading.closest('.faq, .cta-box, .article-next-step-cta, .article-calculator-prompt, .cocoon-box-point'));
            if (headings.length < 3) return;
    
            const tocContainer = document.createElement('nav');
            tocContainer.className = 'inpage-toc';
            tocContainer.setAttribute('aria-label', 'この記事の目次');
    
            const tocTitle = document.createElement('div');
            tocTitle.className = 'inpage-toc-title';
            tocTitle.textContent = '📋 目次';
    
            const tocList = document.createElement('ol');
    
            headings.forEach((heading, index) => {
                if (!heading.id) heading.id = `section-${index + 1}`;
    
                const item = document.createElement('li');
    
                const link = document.createElement('a');
                link.href = `#${heading.id}`;
                // 先頭の「1. 」「2. 」などの連番重複を取り除く
                let cleanText = heading.textContent.replace(/^\s*\d+[\.、．]\s*/u, '').trim();
                cleanText = cleanText.replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}【]+(?![】])/u, '').trim();
                link.textContent = cleanText || heading.textContent.trim();
    
                item.appendChild(link);
                tocList.appendChild(item);
            });
    
            tocContainer.append(tocTitle, tocList);
    
            const pointBox = content.querySelector('.cocoon-box-point');
            const badgeGrid = content.querySelector('.feature-badge-grid');
            const summaryBox = content.querySelector('.summary-box');
            const intro = content.querySelector('.intro');
            const anchor = badgeGrid || pointBox || summaryBox || intro;
            if (anchor) {
                anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', tocContainer);
                return;
            }
            headings[0].insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', tocContainer);
        }
    
        ensureCommonStyles();
        void ensureAnalyticsCore();
        applyArticlePresentationSettings();
    
        // Execute functions

            scheduleCommonAnalytics();
            setupBlogAdsense();
            renderCommonComponents();
            generateTableOfContents();
        });

        'use strict';
    
        const GA_MEASUREMENT_ID = 'G-HED6D0FR4L';
        const ADSENSE_CLIENT = 'ca-pub-3845885843809455';
        let blogAdsenseLoaded = false;
        let consentManagerPromise = null;
        let analyticsCorePromise = null;
    
        const isArticlePageTop = window.location.pathname.includes('/articles/');
        const isBlogPage = window.location.pathname.includes('/blog');
        const isLatestPage = window.location.pathname.includes('/latest/');
        const rootPath = (isArticlePageTop || isBlogPage || isLatestPage) ? '../' : './';
    
        function ensureConsentManager() {
            if (window.PlayPointConsent) return Promise.resolve(window.PlayPointConsent);
            if (consentManagerPromise) return consentManagerPromise;
    
            consentManagerPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="/js/consent.js?v=937d4b14a9"]');
                if (existing) {
                    existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointConsent), { once: true });
                    existing.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                    return;
                }
                const script = document.createElement('script');
                script.src = rootPath + 'js/consent.js?v=937d4b14a9';
                script.async = true;
                script.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointConsent), { once: true });
                script.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                document.head.appendChild(script);
            });
            return consentManagerPromise;
        }
    
        function ensureAnalyticsCore() {
            if (window.PlayPointAnalytics) return Promise.resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics);
            if (analyticsCorePromise) return analyticsCorePromise;
    
            analyticsCorePromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="/js/analytics-core.js?v=cdbab4eb3b"]');
                if (existing) {
                    existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics), { once: true });
                    existing.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                    return;
                }
                const script = document.createElement('script');
                script.src = rootPath + 'js/analytics-core.js?v=cdbab4eb3b';
                script.async = true;
                script.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics), { once: true });
                script.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                document.head.appendChild(script);
            });
            return analyticsCorePromise;
        }
    
        function runAfterConsent(callback, purpose = 'analytics') {
            return Promise.all([ensureAnalyticsCore(), ensureConsentManager()])
                .then(() => {
                    const consent = window.PlayPointConsent;
                    if (purpose === 'ads' && typeof consent.whenAdsAllowed === 'function') {
                        consent.whenAdsAllowed(callback);
                        return;
                    }
                    if (typeof consent.whenAnalyticsGranted === 'function') {
                        consent.whenAnalyticsGranted(callback);
                        return;
                    }
                    consent.whenGranted(callback);
                })
                .catch((error) => console.error('Consent manager load failed:', error));
        }
    
        function getDisplayMode() {
            if (typeof window.matchMedia === 'function' && window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches) {
                return 'standalone';
            }
            if (window.navigator && window.navigator.standalone === true) {
                return 'standalone';
            }
            return 'browser';
        }
    
        // 記事ページにもブログで選んだテーマとカテゴリー配色を引き継ぐ
        function applyArticlePresentationSettings() {
            if (!window.location.pathname.includes('/articles/')) return;
    
            let theme = 'dark';
            try {
                const savedSettings = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('katakata_blog_settings') || '{}');
                if (savedSettings.theme === 'light' || savedSettings.theme === 'dark') {
                    theme = savedSettings.theme;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('ブログ設定を読み込めませんでした。既定テーマを使用します。', error);
            }
    
            const allowedCategories = ['ランク', 'トラブル', '使い方', 'キャンペーン'];
            const category = document.querySelector('meta[name="article:category"]')?.content;
            document.body.dataset.blogTheme = theme;
            document.body.dataset.articleCategory = allowedCategories.includes(category) ? category : '使い方';
        }
    
        // ブログと記事のイベントキューをGA4へ送れるよう、初期描画後に一度だけ読み込む
        function loadCommonAnalytics() {
            if (window.__playpointGaConfigured) return;
            window.__playpointGaConfigured = true;
            window.PlayPointAnalytics.installGtagBridge();
            window.gtag('js', new Date());
            window.gtag('set', { app_display_mode: getDisplayMode() });
            window.gtag('config', GA_MEASUREMENT_ID);
            window.PlayPointAnalytics.markAnalyticsReady();
    
            if (document.querySelector(`script[src*="googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_MEASUREMENT_ID}"]`)) return;
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.async = true;
            script.src = `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_MEASUREMENT_ID}`;
            script.onerror = () => console.error('Analytics load failed');
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        }
    
        function scheduleCommonAnalytics() {
            if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
                window.requestIdleCallback(() => void runAfterConsent(loadCommonAnalytics), { timeout: 2500 });
                return;
            }
            window.setTimeout(() => void runAfterConsent(loadCommonAnalytics), 1500);
        }
    
        function loadBlogAdsense() {
            if (blogAdsenseLoaded) return;
            blogAdsenseLoaded = true;
            if (document.querySelector('script[src*="pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js"]')) return;
    
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.async = true;
            script.src = `https://pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js?client=${ADSENSE_CLIENT}`;
            script.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
            script.onerror = () => {
                blogAdsenseLoaded = false;
                console.error('AdSense load failed');
            };
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        }
    
        function setupBlogAdsense() {
            if (!(isBlogPage || isArticlePageTop)) return;
            // Google Privacy & Messaging / TCFの初期化に必要な非同期ライブラリは早期取得する。
            // 手動広告枠のpushはarticle.js側でad_storage許可後にだけ実行する。
            loadBlogAdsense();
        }
    
        function ensureCommonStyles() {
            if (document.querySelector('link[data-common-components-style]')) return;
            const stylesheet = document.createElement('link');
            stylesheet.rel = 'stylesheet';
            stylesheet.href = rootPath + 'blog/common-components.css?v=bf40a1b867';
            stylesheet.dataset.commonComponentsStyle = 'true';
            document.head.appendChild(stylesheet);
        }
    
        // ===========================================
        // Common Header & Footer
        // ===========================================
        function renderCommonComponents() {
            // ページ固有のヘッダー・フッターがあれば、そのデザインを優先して重複生成しない
            const existingHeader = document.querySelector('header');
            const existingFooter = document.querySelector('footer');
    
            // Allow relative path adjustment if needed (default to absolute/root-relative for now based on file analysis)
            // Since files are in /articles/, links need to go up one level or use absolute URLs
            // The existing code used absolute URLs (https://playpoint-sim.com/), we will preserve that for consistency with current production
            // OR use relative paths if running locally. Let's stick to the existing absolute URLs as seen in the file,
            // BUT for local development, it might be better to handle relative paths.
            // Let's use relative paths for better local preview support, based on where the script is running.
    
            // --- Header Rendering ---
            if (!existingHeader) {
                const headerHTML = `
                <div class="header-inner">
                    <a class="logo" href="${rootPath}index.html">🎮 Playポイント計算機</a>
                    <nav class="nav">
                        <a href="${rootPath}blog/">📝 記事一覧</a>
                        <a href="https://katakatalab.com/">🧪 KatakataLab</a>
                    </nav>
                </div>
                `;
                const header = document.createElement('header');
                header.className = 'header';
                header.innerHTML = headerHTML;
                document.body.insertBefore(header, document.body.firstChild);
            }
    
            // --- Footer Rendering ---
            if (!existingFooter) {
                const footerHTML = `
                <p style="margin-bottom: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.8rem;">
                    <a href="${rootPath}privacy.html">プライバシーポリシー</a> ｜
                    <a href="${rootPath}terms.html">利用規約</a>
                </p>
                <p>© <span class="copyright-year">2024-${new Date().getFullYear()}</span> Playポイント計算機</p>
                `;
                const footer = document.createElement('footer');
                footer.className = 'site-footer';
                footer.innerHTML = footerHTML;
                document.body.appendChild(footer);
            }
        }
    
        // ===========================================
        // Auto Table of Contents (TOC)
        // ===========================================
        function generateTableOfContents() {
            const content = document.querySelector('.content, .main-content-column');
            if (!content || content.querySelector('.toc-box, .inpage-toc, .intl-article-toc, .toc-container, .article-toc, [aria-label*="目次"], [aria-label*="Table of contents"]')) return;
    
            // 主題となるセクションだけに絞り、CTAや補足見出しで目次を膨らませない
            const headings = Array.from(content.querySelectorAll('.section > h2, article > section > h2'))
                .filter(heading => !heading.closest('.faq, .cta-box, .article-next-step-cta, .article-calculator-prompt, .cocoon-box-point'));
            if (headings.length < 3) return;
    
            const tocContainer = document.createElement('nav');
            tocContainer.className = 'inpage-toc';
            tocContainer.setAttribute('aria-label', 'この記事の目次');
    
            const tocTitle = document.createElement('div');
            tocTitle.className = 'inpage-toc-title';
            tocTitle.textContent = '📋 目次';
    
            const tocList = document.createElement('ol');
    
            headings.forEach((heading, index) => {
                if (!heading.id) heading.id = `section-${index + 1}`;
    
                const item = document.createElement('li');
    
                const link = document.createElement('a');
                link.href = `#${heading.id}`;
                // 先頭の「1. 」「2. 」などの連番重複を取り除く
                let cleanText = heading.textContent.replace(/^\s*\d+[\.、．]\s*/u, '').trim();
                cleanText = cleanText.replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}【]+(?![】])/u, '').trim();
                link.textContent = cleanText || heading.textContent.trim();
    
                item.appendChild(link);
                tocList.appendChild(item);
            });
    
            tocContainer.append(tocTitle, tocList);
    
            const pointBox = content.querySelector('.cocoon-box-point');
            const badgeGrid = content.querySelector('.feature-badge-grid');
            const summaryBox = content.querySelector('.summary-box');
            const intro = content.querySelector('.intro');
            const anchor = badgeGrid || pointBox || summaryBox || intro;
            if (anchor) {
                anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', tocContainer);
                return;
            }
            headings[0].insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', tocContainer);
        }
    
        ensureCommonStyles();
        void ensureAnalyticsCore();
        applyArticlePresentationSettings();
    
        // Execute functions
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            scheduleCommonAnalytics();
            setupBlogAdsense();
            renderCommonComponents();
            generateTableOfContents();
        });
    
    })();

    'use strict';
    
    (() => {
        if (window.PlayPointConsent) return;
    
        const analyticsCallbacks = new Set();
        const adCallbacks = new Set();
        const EEA_UK_CH_REGIONS = [
            'AT', 'BE', 'BG', 'CH', 'CY', 'CZ', 'DE', 'DK', 'EE', 'ES', 'FI', 'FR',
            'GB', 'GR', 'HR', 'HU', 'IE', 'IS', 'IT', 'LI', 'LT', 'LU', 'LV', 'MT',
            'NL', 'NO', 'PL', 'PT', 'RO', 'SE', 'SI', 'SK'
        ];
        const PURPOSE_STATUS = Object.freeze({ UNKNOWN: 0, GRANTED: 1, DENIED: 2, NOT_APPLICABLE: 3, NOT_CONFIGURED: 4 });
        const DENIED_STATE = Object.freeze({
            analytics_storage: 'denied',
            ad_storage: 'denied',
            ad_user_data: 'denied',
            ad_personalization: 'denied'
        });
    
        let analyticsStatus = 'pending';
        let adStatus = 'pending';
        let source = 'pending';
        let consentState = DENIED_STATE;
        let tcfListenerAttached = false;
        let timeoutId = null;
    
        // Google tags should read the CMP's TC string directly. This must be set before gtag calls.
        window.gtag_enable_tcf_support = true;
        window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
        window.gtag = window.gtag || function gtag() {
            window.dataLayer.push(arguments);
        };
    
        // Outside regulated regions, keep the existing granted default. In EEA/UK/CH, default to denied
        // until Google Privacy & Messaging / TCF provides the user's choice.
        window.gtag('consent', 'default', {
            analytics_storage: 'granted',
            ad_storage: 'granted',
            ad_user_data: 'granted',
            ad_personalization: 'granted'
        });
        window.gtag('consent', 'default', {
            analytics_storage: 'denied',
            ad_storage: 'denied',
            ad_user_data: 'denied',
            ad_personalization: 'denied',
            region: EEA_UK_CH_REGIONS,
            wait_for_update: 500
        });
    
        function flush(callbacks, allowed) {
            if (!allowed) return;
            for (const callback of callbacks) {
                try { callback(); } catch (error) { console.error('同意後の処理に失敗しました。', error); }
            }
            callbacks.clear();
        }
    
        function dispatchStatus() {
            document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('playpoint:consent-updated', {
                detail: {
                    status: analyticsStatus,
                    adStatus,
                    source,
                    consent: { ...consentState }
                }
            }));
        }
    
        function normalizePurposeStatus(value) {
            if (value === PURPOSE_STATUS.GRANTED || value === PURPOSE_STATUS.NOT_APPLICABLE || value === PURPOSE_STATUS.NOT_CONFIGURED) {
                return 'granted';
            }
            if (value === PURPOSE_STATUS.DENIED) return 'denied';
            return 'pending';
        }
    
        function applyConsentState(nextState, nextSource) {
            consentState = Object.freeze({ ...DENIED_STATE, ...nextState });
            source = nextSource;
            analyticsStatus = consentState.analytics_storage === 'granted'
                ? 'granted'
                : (consentState.analytics_storage === 'pending' ? 'pending' : 'denied');
            adStatus = consentState.ad_storage === 'granted'
                ? 'granted'
                : (consentState.ad_storage === 'pending' ? 'pending' : 'denied');
            if (analyticsStatus !== 'pending' || adStatus !== 'pending') {
                if (timeoutId !== null) window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
                timeoutId = null;
            }
            flush(analyticsCallbacks, analyticsStatus === 'granted');
            flush(adCallbacks, adStatus === 'granted');
            dispatchStatus();
        }
    
        function readGoogleFcConsentMode() {
            if (!window.googlefc || typeof window.googlefc.getGoogleConsentModeValues !== 'function') return false;
            let values;
            try {
                values = window.googlefc.getGoogleConsentModeValues();
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('Google consent mode values could not be read.', error);
                return false;
            }
            if (!values) return false;
            applyConsentState({
                analytics_storage: normalizePurposeStatus(values.analyticsStoragePurposeConsentStatus),
                ad_storage: normalizePurposeStatus(values.adStoragePurposeConsentStatus),
                ad_user_data: normalizePurposeStatus(values.adUserDataPurposeConsentStatus),
                ad_personalization: normalizePurposeStatus(values.adPersonalizationPurposeConsentStatus)
            }, 'googlefc');
            return true;
        }
    
        function buildTcfFallbackState(tcData) {
            if (tcData.gdprApplies === false) {
                return {
                    analytics_storage: 'granted',
                    ad_storage: 'granted',
                    ad_user_data: 'granted',
                    ad_personalization: 'granted'
                };
            }
            const consents = tcData.purpose?.consents || {};
            const purpose1 = consents[1] === true;
            const purpose3 = consents[3] === true;
            const purpose4 = consents[4] === true;
            const purpose7 = consents[7] === true;
            return {
                analytics_storage: purpose1 ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                ad_storage: purpose1 ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                ad_user_data: purpose1 && purpose7 ? 'granted' : 'denied',
                ad_personalization: purpose1 && purpose3 && purpose4 ? 'granted' : 'denied'
            };
        }
    
        function handleTcfData(tcData, success) {
            if (!success || !tcData) return;
            if (!['tcloaded', 'useractioncomplete'].includes(tcData.eventStatus)) return;
            // Google Privacy & Messaging values are the preferred source because they already map
            // the CMP decision to Consent Mode purposes. TCF is a conservative fallback only.
            if (readGoogleFcConsentMode()) return;
            applyConsentState(buildTcfFallbackState(tcData), 'tcf-fallback');
        }
    
        function attachTcfListener() {
            if (tcfListenerAttached || typeof window.__tcfapi !== 'function') return false;
            tcfListenerAttached = true;
            window.__tcfapi('addEventListener', 2, handleTcfData);
            return true;
        }
    
        function registerGoogleFcListener() {
            window.googlefc = window.googlefc || {};
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue = window.googlefc.callbackQueue || [];
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue.push({
                CONSENT_MODE_DATA_READY() {
                    readGoogleFcConsentMode();
                }
            });
        }
    
        function waitForTcfApi() {
            if (attachTcfListener()) return;
            let attempts = 0;
            const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
                attempts += 1;
                if (attachTcfListener() || attempts >= 20) window.clearInterval(timer);
            }, 250);
        }
    
        function showSettings() {
            window.googlefc = window.googlefc || {};
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue = window.googlefc.callbackQueue || [];
            window.googlefc.callbackQueue.push({
                CONSENT_API_READY() {
                    if (typeof window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage === 'function') window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage();
                }
            });
            if (typeof window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage === 'function') window.googlefc.showRevocationMessage();
        }
    
        window.PlayPointConsent = Object.freeze({
            whenAnalyticsGranted(callback) {
                if (typeof callback !== 'function') return;
                if (analyticsStatus === 'granted') callback();
                else analyticsCallbacks.add(callback);
            },
            whenAdsAllowed(callback) {
                if (typeof callback !== 'function') return;
                if (adStatus === 'granted') callback();
                else adCallbacks.add(callback);
            },
            // Backward-compatible alias for analytics callers.
            whenGranted(callback) { this.whenAnalyticsGranted(callback); },
            getStatus() { return analyticsStatus; },
            getAdStatus() { return adStatus; },
            getConsentState() { return { ...consentState }; },
            getSource() { return source; },
            showSettings
        });
    
        registerGoogleFcListener();
        waitForTcfApi();
        // If both the GoogleFC and TCF APIs fail to become available, do not silently grant consent.
        // A late CMP callback can still recover from this denied state.
        timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
            if (analyticsStatus === 'pending' && adStatus === 'pending') applyConsentState(DENIED_STATE, 'timeout');
        }, 5000);
    
        document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('playpoint:consent-ready', {
            detail: { status: analyticsStatus, adStatus }
        }));
    })();

    (function () {
        'use strict';
    
        const GA_MEASUREMENT_ID = 'G-HED6D0FR4L';
        const ADSENSE_CLIENT = 'ca-pub-3845885843809455';
        let blogAdsenseLoaded = false;
        let consentManagerPromise = null;
        let analyticsCorePromise = null;
    
        const isArticlePageTop = window.location.pathname.includes('/articles/');
        const isBlogPage = window.location.pathname.includes('/blog');
        const isLatestPage = window.location.pathname.includes('/latest/');
        const rootPath = (isArticlePageTop || isBlogPage || isLatestPage) ? '../' : './';
    
        function ensureConsentManager() {
            if (window.PlayPointConsent) return Promise.resolve(window.PlayPointConsent);
            if (consentManagerPromise) return consentManagerPromise;
    
            consentManagerPromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="/js/consent.js?v=937d4b14a9"]');
                if (existing) {
                    existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointConsent), { once: true });
                    existing.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                    return;
                }
                const script = document.createElement('script');
                script.src = rootPath + 'js/consent.js?v=937d4b14a9';
                script.async = true;
                script.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointConsent), { once: true });
                script.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                document.head.appendChild(script);
            });
            return consentManagerPromise;
        }
    
        function ensureAnalyticsCore() {
            if (window.PlayPointAnalytics) return Promise.resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics);
            if (analyticsCorePromise) return analyticsCorePromise;
    
            analyticsCorePromise = new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                const existing = document.querySelector('script[src*="/js/analytics-core.js?v=cdbab4eb3b"]');
                if (existing) {
                    existing.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics), { once: true });
                    existing.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                    return;
                }
                const script = document.createElement('script');
                script.src = rootPath + 'js/analytics-core.js?v=cdbab4eb3b';
                script.async = true;
                script.addEventListener('load', () => resolve(window.PlayPointAnalytics), { once: true });
                script.addEventListener('error', reject, { once: true });
                document.head.appendChild(script);
            });
            return analyticsCorePromise;
        }
    
        function runAfterConsent(callback, purpose = 'analytics') {
            return Promise.all([ensureAnalyticsCore(), ensureConsentManager()])
                .then(() => {
                    const consent = window.PlayPointConsent;
                    if (purpose === 'ads' && typeof consent.whenAdsAllowed === 'function') {
                        consent.whenAdsAllowed(callback);
                        return;
                    }
                    if (typeof consent.whenAnalyticsGranted === 'function') {
                        consent.whenAnalyticsGranted(callback);
                        return;
                    }
                    consent.whenGranted(callback);
                })
                .catch((error) => console.error('Consent manager load failed:', error));
        }
    
        function getDisplayMode() {
            if (typeof window.matchMedia === 'function' && window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches) {
                return 'standalone';
            }
            if (window.navigator && window.navigator.standalone === true) {
                return 'standalone';
            }
            return 'browser';
        }
    
        // 記事ページにもブログで選んだテーマとカテゴリー配色を引き継ぐ
        function applyArticlePresentationSettings() {
            if (!window.location.pathname.includes('/articles/')) return;
    
            let theme = 'dark';
            try {
                const savedSettings = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('katakata_blog_settings') || '{}');
                if (savedSettings.theme === 'light' || savedSettings.theme === 'dark') {
                    theme = savedSettings.theme;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.warn('ブログ設定を読み込めませんでした。既定テーマを使用します。', error);
            }
    
            const allowedCategories = ['ランク', 'トラブル', '使い方', 'キャンペーン'];
            const category = document.querySelector('meta[name="article:category"]')?.content;
            document.body.dataset.blogTheme = theme;
            document.body.dataset.articleCategory = allowedCategories.includes(category) ? category : '使い方';
        }
    
        // ブログと記事のイベントキューをGA4へ送れるよう、初期描画後に一度だけ読み込む
        function loadCommonAnalytics() {
            if (window.__playpointGaConfigured) return;
            window.__playpointGaConfigured = true;
            window.PlayPointAnalytics.installGtagBridge();
            window.gtag('js', new Date());
            window.gtag('set', { app_display_mode: getDisplayMode() });
            window.gtag('config', GA_MEASUREMENT_ID);
            window.PlayPointAnalytics.markAnalyticsReady();
    
            if (document.querySelector(`script[src*="googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_MEASUREMENT_ID}"]`)) return;
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.async = true;
            script.src = `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${GA_MEASUREMENT_ID}`;
            script.onerror = () => console.error('Analytics load failed');
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        }
    
        function scheduleCommonAnalytics() {
            if ('requestIdleCallback' in window) {
                window.requestIdleCallback(() => void runAfterConsent(loadCommonAnalytics), { timeout: 2500 });
                return;
            }
            window.setTimeout(() => void runAfterConsent(loadCommonAnalytics), 1500);
        }
    
        function loadBlogAdsense() {
            if (blogAdsenseLoaded) return;
            blogAdsenseLoaded = true;
            if (document.querySelector('script[src*="pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js"]')) return;
    
            const script = document.createElement('script');
            script.async = true;
            script.src = `https://pagead2.googlesyndication.com/pagead/js/adsbygoogle.js?client=${ADSENSE_CLIENT}`;
            script.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
            script.onerror = () => {
                blogAdsenseLoaded = false;
                console.error('AdSense load failed');
            };
            document.head.appendChild(script);
        }
    
        function setupBlogAdsense() {
            if (!(isBlogPage || isArticlePageTop)) return;
            // Google Privacy & Messaging / TCFの初期化に必要な非同期ライブラリは早期取得する。
            // 手動広告枠のpushはarticle.js側でad_storage許可後にだけ実行する。
            loadBlogAdsense();
        }
    
        function ensureCommonStyles() {
            if (document.querySelector('link[data-common-components-style]')) return;
            const stylesheet = document.createElement('link');
            stylesheet.rel = 'stylesheet';
            stylesheet.href = rootPath + 'blog/common-components.css?v=bf40a1b867';
            stylesheet.dataset.commonComponentsStyle = 'true';
            document.head.appendChild(stylesheet);
        }
    
        // ===========================================
        // Common Header & Footer
        // ===========================================
        function renderCommonComponents() {
            // ページ固有のヘッダー・フッターがあれば、そのデザインを優先して重複生成しない
            const existingHeader = document.querySelector('header');
            const existingFooter = document.querySelector('footer');
    
            // Allow relative path adjustment if needed (default to absolute/root-relative for now based on file analysis)
            // Since files are in /articles/, links need to go up one level or use absolute URLs
            // The existing code used absolute URLs (https://playpoint-sim.com/), we will preserve that for consistency with current production
            // OR use relative paths if running locally. Let's stick to the existing absolute URLs as seen in the file,
            // BUT for local development, it might be better to handle relative paths.
            // Let's use relative paths for better local preview support, based on where the script is running.
    
            // --- Header Rendering ---
            if (!existingHeader) {
                const headerHTML = `
                <div class="header-inner">
                    <a class="logo" href="${rootPath}index.html">🎮 Playポイント計算機</a>
                    <nav class="nav">
                        <a href="${rootPath}blog/">📝 記事一覧</a>
                        <a href="https://katakatalab.com/">🧪 KatakataLab</a>
                    </nav>
                </div>
                `;
                const header = document.createElement('header');
                header.className = 'header';
                header.innerHTML = headerHTML;
                document.body.insertBefore(header, document.body.firstChild);
            }
    
            // --- Footer Rendering ---
            if (!existingFooter) {
                const footerHTML = `
                <p style="margin-bottom: 0.5rem; font-size: 0.8rem;">
                    <a href="${rootPath}privacy.html">プライバシーポリシー</a> ｜
                    <a href="${rootPath}terms.html">利用規約</a>
                </p>
                <p>© <span class="copyright-year">2024-${new Date().getFullYear()}</span> Playポイント計算機</p>
                `;
                const footer = document.createElement('footer');
                footer.className = 'site-footer';
                footer.innerHTML = footerHTML;
                document.body.appendChild(footer);
            }
        }
    
        // ===========================================
        // Auto Table of Contents (TOC)
        // ===========================================
        function generateTableOfContents() {
            const content = document.querySelector('.content, .main-content-column');
            if (!content || content.querySelector('.toc-box, .inpage-toc, .intl-article-toc, .toc-container, .article-toc, [aria-label*="目次"], [aria-label*="Table of contents"]')) return;
    
            // 主題となるセクションだけに絞り、CTAや補足見出しで目次を膨らませない
            const headings = Array.from(content.querySelectorAll('.section > h2, article > section > h2'))
                .filter(heading => !heading.closest('.faq, .cta-box, .article-next-step-cta, .article-calculator-prompt, .cocoon-box-point'));
            if (headings.length < 3) return;
    
            const tocContainer = document.createElement('nav');
            tocContainer.className = 'inpage-toc';
            tocContainer.setAttribute('aria-label', 'この記事の目次');
    
            const tocTitle = document.createElement('div');
            tocTitle.className = 'inpage-toc-title';
            tocTitle.textContent = '📋 目次';
    
            const tocList = document.createElement('ol');
    
            headings.forEach((heading, index) => {
                if (!heading.id) heading.id = `section-${index + 1}`;
    
                const item = document.createElement('li');
    
                const link = document.createElement('a');
                link.href = `#${heading.id}`;
                // 先頭の「1. 」「2. 」などの連番重複を取り除く
                let cleanText = heading.textContent.replace(/^\s*\d+[\.、．]\s*/u, '').trim();
                cleanText = cleanText.replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}【]+(?![】])/u, '').trim();
                link.textContent = cleanText || heading.textContent.trim();
    
                item.appendChild(link);
                tocList.appendChild(item);
            });
    
            tocContainer.append(tocTitle, tocList);
    
            const pointBox = content.querySelector('.cocoon-box-point');
            const badgeGrid = content.querySelector('.feature-badge-grid');
            const summaryBox = content.querySelector('.summary-box');
            const intro = content.querySelector('.intro');
            const anchor = badgeGrid || pointBox || summaryBox || intro;
            if (anchor) {
                anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', tocContainer);
                return;
            }
            headings[0].insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin', tocContainer);
        }
    
        ensureCommonStyles();
        void ensureAnalyticsCore();
        applyArticlePresentationSettings();
    
        // Execute functions
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            scheduleCommonAnalytics();
            setupBlogAdsense();
            renderCommonComponents();
            generateTableOfContents();
        });
    
    })();
